**Known EA script errors (2026-10-25, harness):** hotfix gate `ip-hf-matrix` reload failed on EA's wedding NRE (`WeddingCeremony:CleanUp` <- `Situation:Exit` <- `RouteToWeddingArch:OnRouteFailed`, Sparkling Sands, Baseline-IP; 27 probe-out runs since 2026-10-12). `tools/harness/known-script-errors.json` + `.ts` (all-EA stacks only), used by `save-matrix.ts` reload: known errors reported `known EA error <id> (ignored)`, never counted as new. Rerun the reload case to confirm.

Approved 2026-10-10. Rewritten 2026-10-12 with everything found so far (read this first after a context reset).

Goal: The Sims 3 (Steam 1.67, all EPs/SPs) with **less in-game stutter** first (stuck Sims, routing, sim-thread work; load times are fine), then fewer crashes (Error 12 / out of address space) and room for heavy CC. Rodney wants every avenue tried. Workflow: online prior art → reverse engineering → rank big wins → build, one change at a time, measured with the harness.

**Long-term goal: offload the sim thread (Rodney, 2026-10-14).** Move everything that can safely leave the game's single busy core (the sim thread) to other cores, so longer saves, more objects and more Sims scale instead of hitting that one core's limit. This needs a major architecture redesign and is a multi-step, long-term track (roadmap: docs/23 #17). Acceptance rule for offload changes: a change that moves measurable work off the sim thread is accepted even with no speed gain today, or a loss of up to ~2% in sim iterations/s, as long as it is stable (no crashes, no new stuck Sims, same results). Measure it as sim-thread ms/s moved off (route ms/s, stage ms/s), not only iterations/s. Losses over ~2% need a plan to win them back.

**PLAN RETARGETED TO THE NEW TEST METHOD (Rodney 2026-10-23; read this first).** How we work now:
1. **Discover** real player hitches only from natural play (real-hitch classifier: docs/161 §6; visible freezes + frames: docs/162/167) and the story fuzzer at night (docs/171). A natural run is discovery or a final per-release check, never a fix's test.
2. **Name the cause** per hitch (stage, script task, wait key, lock holder, sync-plan phase, frame service), then **force it**: a repro loop in the real save that fires the cause many times per load (shortcut style, docs/169), setup steps marked.
3. **Fix and test in minutes:** verify job (short, shadow compares) + loop A/B in one load, ABBA, >= 10 firings per arm, fixed event-count stop, confidence intervals (docs/168).
4. **Moved-waits loop:** read the A/B's moved-waits table; every cause that GREW in the on arm is the next target, same repro, fixes combined into one arm until the arm is clean (memory moved-waits-loop).
5. **Release:** `tools/release-check.ts` (smoke, save matrix, one forced loop per shipped fix, bundle A/B on repros, soak), ~2.5-3 h; no long night pairs.
Current targets in that order: (a) the lock-taker bundle for SV/IP 1-2 s freezes: SteerRO+AvoidRO+Gate0RO+DeferFootprint(2)+RouteToObjRO (steer-pass-ab.json, docs/175) + PlacementRO (docs/174, build after the chain) + ForeignKinds (docs/176): verify, then one bundle loop A/B, then the moved-waits loop; (b) SV 7 s crowd freeze: sv-crowd-cause run, then its repro; (c) overwatchchain (BP's only remaining >= 300 ms natural hitch); (d) crash 0x567121 reruns, then an Apex issue/PR if it is the game's own bug; (e) go/no-go study of a structural fix for the +0x918 lock (read-only routing snapshot, design E docs/82) since single read-only fixes keep moving the wait; (f) story fuzzer merge + smoke + believability lint; (g) Tuesday release via release-check. Superseded: fixed 10-min night pairs, natural-play A/Bs, ms/s-only targets.

**Hang capture before the kill (docs/172 §6, ledger §281; built 2026-10-23, self-check only, NOT run):** on a heartbeat freeze (`watchGame`) or a stall guard's "nothing moved" with the game and probe up, `game-alive.ts` `captureHang` writes to `probe-out/queue/<tag>-hang/` before the FAIL line and the kill: `/simstack` x5 at 1 s, `/threadstack?which=all`, `/native`, the `ts3native_lockholders` ring (agent.js `/nativeexport` now returns the address), `/managedstack` (skipped if a sim sample timed out), and a procdump minidump (`TS3_HANG_DUMP=off|mini|mp|ma`, default mini; skipped when procdump is not installed). **Sync plan deadline + fail memo built 2026-10-22 (docs/164 §7 D1-D5, ledger §270; `game-copy/ts3native.asi.0332` = asi.0331 + this, ts3native 0.36.27, counters v99, sha256 `2F17629B93032D8E2FC31E0FC05E284176D6742ABBA1E7A380F7CE4DABF6CEF6`, NOT run):** `[RouteAsync] PlanDeadlineMs` gives every sim-thread sync plan (route IC sync path incl. goal step; sim `Route_PlanLocked` outside it, e.g. Replan with A10 off) one wall deadline; past it world A* drains through the cost stubs (also every later goal-lot / open-ground candidate), cell A* `0x61E500` and water `0x671930` take their own no-path exits at entry (lot legs fail through cell A*); portal A*, `LotPlan`, builds and the goal step are not cut. Own-task IC plans are never cut unless `PlanDeadlineOwnMs` is set. `FailMemo` = ReplanDedupe's failed-repeat answer on the sync Plan IC. **Several repros per load + session reuse (Rodney 2026-10-22: cut test time; docs/170 §1-2, ledger §272; self-check + dry-run, NOT run):** a profile job's `repros: [...]` runs those repros back to back in one load, one window each with its own tag `<job tag>-<id>`, its own pass rules and its own ts3native.log slice. `reuseSession` (default off) lets consecutive profile jobs with the same game start share the running game. Between windows every ts3fixes and ts3native switch goes back to its after-load value, and a world snapshot (clock, Sims, household, lot objects, VAS) is checked against limits. The next window gets a fresh start past a limit, after `maxJobs`, or with a stuck Sim. A failure restarts fresh at night and stops the queue in the daytime. The first minute is excluded only after a load (`<tag>-session.json`); otherwise a 15 s start edge. There is one frames session per load. Estimate: ~2-3 min plus 45 s of real data per reused window. Next: run `queues/faster-tests-example.json` once (I-170-11), then a fresh vs reused ABBA (I-170-14) before measurement A/Bs use reuse.

**Holder site 0 fixed 2026-10-22 (docs/165 §8, ledger §265; `game-copy/ts3native.asi.0331` = asi.0330 + this, ts3native 0.36.26, counters v98, sha256 `380f90054a65f4a5de49dce0843aa4e24f8e170205f4c962dedfaca41498f152`, NOT run):** the `asi.0330` Holders run logged 2 of 4 waits with holder site 0 (AsyncLoader `[this+0x40]` and ResourceMan `GetPackageSource` `[this+0x48]` locks, both passed between threads). Causes: the holder wrote its entry after its own record and log line, the waiter read the table only at wait start, and the entry was cleared on release before a re-acquire; direct-mapped collisions were possible too. Fix: the entry is written first, the waiter reads it again when its acquire returns, entries are never cleared (the release hook has no Holders test now), and the table is 4-way set-associative. Every acquire is `Lock_Acquire 0x4E16F0` (no inlined copies; it returns the new count). Selftest has one holder mode per cause; selftest, steerro x4, hook-sites and check-docs-code pass. Next: one `Holders=1` run on asi.0331, expecting `hoNoSite` near 0.
**F1 lock-hook fast path + lock holders built 2026-10-22 (docs/165 §7, ledger §261; `game-copy/ts3native.asi.0330` = asi.0329 + this, ts3native 0.36.25, counters v98, sha256 `49e006433331ebcdd913f8486e04a46d350dd50a5456500e9fe698bf013479ca`, NOT run):**
- F1: `LockAcquireHook`/`LockReleaseHook` send a lock other than `+0x918`, taken by a thread that is not the job owner or the sync-session thread and holds no QL/SlotEnabledRO hold, straight to the original. There is no key, `GetCurrentThreadId`, SEH or P5b work on that path.
- The owner and `+0x918` paths are unchanged.
- The watchdog's `__try` moved into `LockOwnerTid`.
- Selftest bench (hot cache, noisy machine): hook overhead per non-918 pair went from +2.6..+8.2 ns to +1.0..+1.6 ns. Contended runs show no difference beyond noise.
- `[HitchAttr] Holders=1` (default 0, `HoldersMinMs=20`) records sim and main waits >= 20 ms on any game lock other than `+0x918`, with the holder's thread role and acquire site. Records go to the `ts3native_lockholders` ring, the hitch line's `holders` field (stutter-profile.js) and `worst-hitches.ts cause().holder`. It costs ~3-4 ns per pair while on, and a two-thread selftest covers it.
- Checks passed: full selftest, steerro-test default + PHASES + SYNCPHASES, `hook-sites --check`, `check-docs-code`.
- Next: §5.3 rerun on a clean night (main-thread ts3native share should be <= 1%), plus one `Holders=1` run.

**Main-thread waits 2026-10-22 (docs/165, ledger §260; asi.0329 rebuilt with /MAP (byte-identical), `bp-freeze-pan` mainstack, no game run):** the main thread's ts3native frames are one pass-through chain (`LockAcquireHook+0x1d7` -> `LockAcquireAsync+0x3cc` -> `P5bLock+0x52` -> `g_lockOrig`; `+0x596f0` = SEH handler, not a frame); it never reaches a gate, pump, H2/TailUnlock/P5b or `+0x918` wait. The "callers" `TS3W+0xbfd74c` etc. are `.rdata` EA allocator names (ResourceMan, AsyncLoader, Refpack, Cache): resource-system locks, game's own. Real blocking 1.5% (~0.5 ms/frame; 2.7% with the allocator CS); **our cost = hook overhead 4.9% of main-thread samples (~1.75 ms of a 36 ms frame)**. Next: F1 fast path at the top of `LockAcquireHook`/`LockReleaseHook` for non-owner threads and locks other than `+0x918`, SEH watchdog moved out of the common path (~30-45 min + selftest, est. -1.2..-1.5 ms/frame); size it with a selftest micro-benchmark, optional `[MainLockProbe]` counters (§5.2). Gate exemption for the main thread = 0 gain, do not build. Game lock/allocator traffic (~12 ms/frame) goes to Apex.

**P5 lending gain model 2026-10-22 (docs/158, ledger §240; static + existing run data, `tools/p5-gain-model.ts`, NOT run):** P5 lends only during async ReplanFromPosition jobs, so in the release config (A10 off) every lending arm cuts 0. With A10 RFP on, the window is 17-23% of an eligible job (BP; IP 35%), ~5 ms per job; admitted takers carry 55-60% (P5b) / 86-89% (P5c) of the BP keyed wait, but by conservation the sim uses at most q <= 0.04-0.17 (P5b) / 0.22-0.5 (P5c) of the lent time, so the lending term is <= 0.4 / 3.1 ms/s BP. P5 itself lengthens each eligible job (+3.8..+9.6 ms measured with P5c on 0.35.25; P5b alone +5.6..+13 inferred), which outweighs it: P5b alone +5..+10 ms/s wait (worse, inferred), P5c -6..+7 (measured +3.0 mean), B2 vs B1 +1..+4. **Next (no build):** `snap-p5c-ab.json` on the current asi as day jobs per world with FreeLock=0 and FreeLock=1, read with `node tools/p5-gain-model.ts <tag>`; if dT >= ~3.5 ms per eligible job on both worlds, spend no clean night on P5b/P5c lending (work goes to the take path or the docs/150 hold cuts); only if dT <= 0 build the per-job wait counters of docs/158 §5 (q).
**P5 clone writer audit 2026-10-22 (docs/156, ledger §241; static, nothing built, NOT run):** every writer of every clone search input checked against the replay. One verified unrecorded writer that changes the answer: `Footprint_SetLockKeyObject 0x807110` (lock-key ICs via `0x82B2B0`) sets `fp+0x20` on an inserted footprint with no W1/W2 and no signature change, so a door/portal footprint that gets its first lock-key set keeps "no keys" in the clone (in-room class `0x807090`) until re-insert, full rebase or epoch (I-156-1). Polygon caches: the only valid-byte clear in the image is `0x80E0B0` (W6e), with no caller found, so I-94-381's refill path has no static caller (I-156-2/3); W6d/W6f never change an inserted footprint in place (I-156-4). Worker W6b on live footprints in clone runs is a live-state race (I-156-5). Next: F1 (7-byte stub on `0x807110`, force the footprint's lots full) + F2 (`snW6e` counter and force in the existing W6e row), ~1.5-2 h, one replay.cpp round; then F3 (EnsureAll at the armed cut on live grid/request footprints, ~1 h).

**Lent-window takers 2026-10-22 (docs/155, ledger §242; static, nothing built):** every sim `+0x918` taker re-walked to 5 hops (`tools/p5-lent-walk.py`, unresolved sites in `docs/data/p5-lent-unresolved.csv`) against P5b, P5c and TailUnlock windows and the job's phases. No taker races a window (the worker reads the clone or job-private data). One race after the window [inferred]: gate-12 F-G (`0x631F40`) is admitted for its `Contains` leaf, then runs the pinned quadtree query `0x667480` unlocked, which can overlap the worker's fallback plan or TailUnlock's rest of plan: drop F-G from `P5bGate` (~0.8-1.6 ms/s). P5c's cut-based stale rule misses nothing a window rule would catch; its problem is over-refusal (cache-fill leaves, every parked destroy) plus discourage add's managed event 0x11 under the lent lock with D off; keep `FreeLock` off, P5c-lite design in §8. Gaps: `SetOceanPortalOwner` has no `nr` family; F-C tail vcalls and RouteToObjectCheck write-backs unknown. Unmapped keys `0x729E2C`, `0x728DC3`, `0x7345F2`, `0x4B0021` are not `+0x918`; `0x75A64C` is steering from goal validation. Next: drop F-G (~30 min), tripwires TW-1/3/4/5 (~2 h).

**Cell A\* cache 2026-10-22 (docs/150 §8, ledger §230; static, nothing built):** a cross-plan leg cache is rejected for now: the answer depends on exact points and on the agent (own id, ignore list, options), whole plans almost never repeat (docs/23 §18.1), and walking Sims' footprint writes would churn a per-tree epoch. Node-table reuse would gain nothing because graph nodes already come from the routing pool `0x61AE80`. `Q+0x24` is zeroed per search at `0x61E539` [verified]. Next: a counter-only probe of design M (within-plan leg memo, cleared at Q reset `0x61E320`), ~1-2 h build plus one 10-min day job. Build M only if the would-hit cell A\* time is >= ~5 ms/s.
**Post pass off the hold 2026-10-22 (docs/150 §9, ledger §231; static, nothing built):** the node post pass `0x64E380` is a `+0x918` writer, not job-private: passes 1, 3, 6 reach lot-table inserts (`0x62EAB0`) and the deferred quadtree apply (`0x666A40`), pass 10 the steering obstacle query `0x82EDB0` [verified, direct chains]. **Lock-taker bundle design 2026-10-22 (docs/152, ledger §222; static + existing run files, NOT run):** 15 mapped `+0x918` keys plus 7 unmapped ones (~4-9 ms/s), each mapped 5 hops: RO with balanced writes (steering, gather, avoidance, gate-0 hits, gate-12 reads, `0x83156D`), writers (USF, `0x8334CC`, `0x82D296`, `0x82A0F6`, gate 10, `0x62FB74`), lifetime (destroy). Arm B0 = the existing 7 switches in one `--ab` (`steerro,avoidro,slotenabledro,lotfdro,obstructedro,deferfootprint,destroypark`): predicted ~0 net, because gate 0 G-a and `0x83156D` absorb the removed keys (a cheap falsification of the conservation model). B1 adds the gaps: G1 gate-0 G-a hit under QL shared (safe only because S2/AvoidRO make the callers' next touches RO, 3-5 h), G5 RouteToObjRO (8-12 h), U phase 2 `0x8334A0` (3-5 h), `UpdateObjectLot` (3-5 h) and `RemovePrivateFootprint` (1-2 h) deferrals, mapping the unknown keys (1-2 h), seeding all bundle keys + a bundle report (1-2 h). Total ~20-33 h; forecast BP summed wait ~68-92 -> ~20-35 ms/s, +3-6% it/s [inferred]. B2 = B1 + P5b; P5c stays out (admitted writers make clone answers stale). H2 and asyncreplan stay off. Risks: forced `DiscDrain` under a lent lock, fewer door-ask pumps (`asyncUs` up). **Next:** B0 day verify + one clean pair, then build G1 first.

**Houseboat move / dock edge failures explained 2026-10-22 (docs/143 §8.17, ledger §249; measured, fixed check built, NOT run):** `unseen-houseboat-move` failed with the throttle on (4, 3) and off (2), `unseen-dock-edge` on 5 / off 5: the repro's own Go Here targets were world points at the berth (deck) or on the gangway line into the berth (dock), so after the boat sailed our walk task took Sims off the deck (17 of 17 mid-check rows dry, off-deck Sims on other lots with `GoHere`/`GoHome`) or into the water; the old check also counted Sims that left on their own. Throttle cleared of these failures; stranding itself still open. ts3agent `houseboat_move` fixed: deck targets follow the live lot centre and must be on the lot, dock targets = the placed spots, only Sims on the support and dry at the first move are watched, fail = in water (now or at a walk sample) with no water intent since the move. **Unseen throttle zero gain explained 2026-10-22 (docs/143 §8.15, measured, pair means of both slice orders):** the sim thread's `+0x918` wait is conserved per part (bounded by the route job's hold, ~115 ms/s), the CPU left to save is a few ms/s, and the avoidance skip costs ~10% it/s. Release default (`List=1`, `Avoid=0`, `Footprint=0`, `Rate=0`) keeps a small gain (~+5% it/s BP, route -8.8 ms/s). Real lever for the waits: shorter or lent hold (P5b/P5c, H2, S2); decision for Rodney. Single-order A/B jobs are not usable (slow middle slices). simBusy profiler (2c7294f) crashed frida-agent.dll: reverted in the working copy, needs a dummy-process test before reuse.

**FallbackAsync worst-hitch regression explained 2026-10-21 (docs/134 §10, ledger §214; static + logs):** the SV 1,027 / 541 ms frames of clean-night-0.2.0 are the sim thread waiting out one uncapped worker job (destroy `0x02000003`, steering `0x85FE10`); jobs > 400 ms only with `FallbackAsync=1` (up to 4,638 ms in freeze-fix-ab route-bits). Fix `[RouteAsync] FbJobCapMs` (ts3native 0.36.16, default 0, `--ab fbjobcap`, release-features `fbjobcap` = 300) + `long job` log line; A/B `tools/harness/queues/fbjobcap-ab.json` not run (asi `game-copy/ts3native.asi.0316b`). Recommendation: do not ship FallbackAsync+M1Retry on 0.36.14 as is; ship with FbJobCapMs after its A/B, else FallbackAsync=0 + SyncCapMs=1000.

**docs/130 §9 second static pass 2026-10-20 (no build or run):** all 22 EA superops equal their base sequences (no mismatch); G-I4 closed: static-field helper `0xD89CD0` is ~0.04-0.07% on a live chain, not 1.8% (stale return addresses in the stack scan; first-12-frame inclusive shares are upper bounds, G-I9 too), per-field cache designed but not worth building; G-B4 closed: only 3 `[ThreadStatic]` fields (`AppDomain`), none reached by allow-list v0 or tier 2, no fix; also closed G-B6 (TaskControl IC bodies), G-B7 (allocator lock always set), G-I8, `Interlocked` 64-bit (global lock). **Mint fast paths 2026-10-21 (docs/139, static + monohost, no game run):** EA fuses superops only when a whole straight-line run equals one of its 22 sequences (`0xD89930` hashes the whole window), so `ldthis+ldfld.*` never fuse inside longer runs. Built in monohost: sub-run fusion (5-byte call patch at `0xE5C8CA`, `MONOHOST_MINTFUSE=sub`): dispatches -0.7% (EA `.cctor` code) to -11.8% (bench), pinned bench -7.1%, same checksum; 37/37 in 59 of 60 host runs (54 in a row), one unexplained `36/37` run with the check id lost. Threaded dispatch -1.7% (G-I10 closed, do not build); range-check removal no gain; `callvirt` cache misses 0.15-0.17% offline (two-entry cache and prefetch rejected, G-I5). Ranked: (1) sub-run fusion ~2-8 ms/s [inferred], 1-2 h port as `[MintFuse] Sub=1` default off + 2-load A/B night; (2) call/return fast path via jump-table entries ~15-30 ms/s [inferred], 8-14 h, in-load A/B; (3) new superops after G-I1, ~1-4 ms/s. Ledger docs/51 §194.

New: mint jump table `0xE59880` (449 opcodes) and in-exe name table `0x11949E0` -> `docs/data/mint-opcodes.csv`; EA's 22 superinstructions (`0x1AB`-`0x1C0`, matcher `0xD89930`, peephole `0xE5C660`); calls stay in one C frame (63 records x `0x130`); `callvirt` monomorphic cache; filter clauses abort; `0xD790A0` always under the loader lock; image sections not GC roots in game (static). Samples: interpreter self 25.5%, call/return ~43% of it, hot spot = receiver load in `callvirt`, transform 0 in play. Ledger docs/51 §175. **Next:** fast paths G-I1 (managed sampler via `[[record]+4]+8`, 1 BP job) then G-I2 inliner study; second thread G-B1 (P4 `FrameTls` with TEB expansion slots, 1-2 h build).

**G0 IP clone-run faults triaged, 0.36.2 built 2026-10-20 (docs/94 §22.31, I-94-508, offline):** both IP faults (0.35.24 `0x667F0F`, 0.35.25 `0x668654`, heap eip) call through a freed clone footprint copy from a clone leaf entry whose FME the replayed ApplyRemove freed (leaf entries hold no FME reference, verified). Source: since 0.35.21 a patched REM (child `0xFEBF90` copies wider than live) erased only inside live's rect, while the clone had inserted with the copy's; no lot forced. Morning: fix the job (fixesOn), rerun bp-mono-ledger. bp-autshadow-count PASS but AutShadow made 0 requests (req 0, cuts 0; native ready 15, 1,779 guard slots, 0 aborts; ts3fixes `fixes on: autshadow (verified)`): the ts3fixes side never called TryRequest. Read the ts3fixes applied-lines and counters 122-126 (agent `patches`) for the cause: G-M1 MVID set not accepted, G-M4 swap found, or the B13 hook not installed. ip-autshadow-count and bp-autshadow-r are uninformative until fixed. Lead [inferred, code read]: the ledger scope was also dead (`scope NO`), and both depend on MetaHook; AutShadow.cs installs MetaHook only when every worker hook patched (`sHooksOk = ok && sCalcPatched && Patch(...MetaHook)`), so one failed Patch (likely a new one: Posture.get_Interactions or a LocalizeString overload) leaves B13 unhooked. Check AppliedLog `AutShadow:` lines via the agent first. **bp-vres CRASH 2026-10-21 03:19 PHT (virtual-resolution.json, asi ts3native.asi.vres = 0.36.3 /DTS3N_VCSITES4 + agent vcache):** game died ~60 s into the window (agent not answering 61 s), no new xcpt file. The first `[vres]` snapshot runs at 60 s, so the lead [inferred] is the agent-side mint cache walk (`vcache` in tools/probe/agent.js, never run in game). Not a release or Mono-build issue. Morning: per probe-safety, run the snapshot on a dummy/limited path first (one image, a few methods, budget on), check JS exception handling and pointer guards in the decoder, then rerun bp-vres; consider the native sampler half alone (vres off) to split the two mechanisms. **Cause found 2026-10-21 (docs/133 §5, ledger §189), not a game crash [verified, logs]:** the first snapshot was one synchronous agent RPC that held the script thread; `/agentstatus` queued behind it, game-alive called the game dead after 61 s and the queue closed it, while the int3 sampler kept counting at the same rate for 90 s more. **Mono M5 first in-game smoke 2026-10-20 (bp-mt-gates-off, master 0.35.22 build, noisy):** PASS, no crash. P3 19 sites + ArenaFix + F1 installed (`p3Ready` 23, `n2mReady` 1, `finRuns` 73,809, `arenaRelNonEmpty` 0 in 5 min). **New blocker: P4 FrameTls SKIPPED in game: TlsAlloc returned indexes 102/103, not below 64** (the game process uses >64 TLS slots; the `fs:` rewrite only reaches the 64 inline slots). So no per-thread frame globals in game: the worker must not interpret (AutShadow Mode R jobs dropped from the queue). Fix: use TEB TlsExpansionSlots (`fs:[0xF94]` + (idx-64)*4) in the rebuilt blocks, or allocate the two keys before the game fills the inline slots (DllMain, early). I-120-1 confirmed again (sim thread registered, main not).
**Mono M5 G-A2 2026-10-20 (docs/120 §9, host runs only, no game run):** `0xE5444D` cause found: EA's frame arena pool `[0x11EE508]` got a duplicate chunk because release-all `0xD71B70` leaves `state+0x13C` set and EA frees a finished task state twice (a task ending with an exception pools its chunks twice); two live states then share a chunk. Not a missed interpreter global (core census = P4's two only). Fix `[MonoThread] ArenaFix=1` (with `GcLock=1`): clear after release-all, dedupe at install, drop in-use chunks before grow. F1 live: a host IC through `0xD8A8E0` on both threads crashes with the game's chain; `N2mPrivate=1` (worker-private call records) fixes it. `ts3native_mtprobe` v2. monohost selftest 33/33 x4 final (concurrent jobs now default; job A 5 rounds, job B ~755 main-run collections pushing the worker stack = I-120-15 verified); controls fail as expected. I-120-1 live: Boehm's table holds the sim thread only. Ledger docs/51 §157. **Next:** ts3fixes `MtProbe` job + Verify in game; chain F1 with `[Verify]`'s `0xD8A8E0` stub; measure `arenaRelNonEmpty` in game (I-120-22).

**P3-B child-footprint soft misses, ts3native 0.35.8 (lane G) on master 0.35.7, counters v72, offline, not run (docs/94 §21.23, docs/51 §132, ~2 h):** 0.35.6 BP/IP rebased 865/654 lots (13.8/11.1 ms/s), 693/473 of them soft resync forces, every miss a child footprint with "clone copy: none". No game writer: `0x628220`/`0x665C50` have no pointer anywhere, so every child insert passes the hooked ApplyInsert (I-94-401 refuted). Cause: our `RpKids` deleted the child table entry of a live child address reused by another parent's new child when the old block was retired or freed; selftest (i2) reproduces the live lines on the old code. Now deleted only when it is the block's own child (`rpKidsKept`). Shell-only lot refresh not built (79/75 rebases, all Q list unchanged). Next: `snap-p3b` BP + IP on `game-copy/ts3native.asi.laneG`; pass: 0 mismatches, soft misses and soft rebases near 0, `rpKidsKept` > 0.
**P4 lock-key table release, ts3native 0.35.7 (lane F) on master 0.35.6, counters v71, offline, not run (docs/94 §21.22, docs/51 §131, ~1 h):** IP 0.35.5 refused every shadow `mat`/`copy` from record 1104 on: `PcAcquire` kept every lock-key table for the clone's life and refused at 64; since 0.35.5's soft resync the clone never rebuilds, so the cap never reset (reproduced in selftest at run 63). A full key apply now releases the older tables (only the newest stays). Next: `snap-p4` IP on `game-copy/ts3native.asi.laneF`; pass: no `mat`/`copy` run, 0 M1-M3.

**P4 `nover`/`busy` cut sharing, ts3native 0.35.6 (lane D) on master 0.35.5, counters v71, offline, not run (docs/94 §21.21, docs/51 §130, ~2 h):** 0.35.2's `nover`/`busy` (BP 708, IP 140) is all STARTs while a job runs (`p4QCutMiss` 0). Such a START now shares the newest pending cut (`PcBorrow`): that cut's version and write count, so any write after it makes the record stale (M5); the slot's hold counts both shadows; a first shadow that wrote clone lots refuses the second (`mat`/`stale`). Also `w0` is now read inside the cut's `+0x918` hold (a queued job could write before the old read). New `p4Borrow`, `p4BorrowMiss`, `p4BorrowDirty`. Next: `snap-p4` BP/IP on `game-copy/ts3native.asi.laneD`; pass: `nover`/`busy` far down, `p4Borrow` > 0, 0 M1-M3.

**P3-B soft resync, ts3native 0.35.5 (lane A) on master 0.35.2, counters v70, offline, not run (docs/94 §21.20, docs/51 §129, ~2.5 h):** 0.35.2's `rpHard` put every resync rebuild on "soft tries used up" (BP 76, IP 21). The limit counted failed batches, and batches cut before the consumer's force landed missed the same lot again (I-94-400, inferred). Now only a miss on a lot the batch itself rebased counts toward 3; a miss whose force is pending is in flight (not forced again), others are fresh (forced; 32 failed batches in a row before a rebuild). New `rpSoftReal/Fresh/InFlight`, `rpRebaseWhy[4]`, `rpRebaseQSame` (sizes a shell-only lot refresh, not built). The unrecorded child write itself is open (I-94-401). Next: `snap-p3b` BP/IP on `game-copy/ts3native.asi.laneA`; pass: `rpHard[1]` near 0, far fewer builds, 0 audit mismatches.

**`TestP5cIn` flake, ts3native 0.35.7 on master 0.35.5, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.12, docs/51 §134, ~2.5 h):** the bad-at-7 save failure is a test race: the fake worker started jobs without ServeAsync's `g_rgOffSimOpen` Dekker check, and the test's save and destroy skipped `RtEnter`'s hold. Fixed in the test. Also, a ServeAsync back-off now signals `g_asyncDone`, so the owner no longer waits for its 2 s timeout. 0 P5c failures in 160 fixed runs. Open: the line-316 hand-over failure was not reproduced (the test now prints its four values); a CPU-bound hang on master under 4-lane load (2 of about 60 runs); the P4 flake at selftest.cpp:7599 (about 8%), which blocks a run of 50 clean selftests in a row. P5c stays off live until the line-316 values have been seen once or 50 clean runs pass.

**Selftest 50-clean gate, ts3native 0.35.11 on master 0.35.10, tests only, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.14, docs/51 §135, ~1.5 h):** every failure was in the tests. P4 M1 (about 8-10%): the queued-STARTs phase wrote `RfStep`'s unrecorded kinds while the other job sat between its cut and live plan (with that change off: M1 in 4 of 40, all in that phase); now recorded writes only then. `TestP5cIn` hand-over: seen once under load as `hand at -1`, hand-overs +0 (the tripwire never fired in jobs 380-389, no P5c fault); jobs now go on until it fires. P5b steering ratio (most loaded runs) now counts only waits started while the window is open; rare P5b classes go first in each phase; queued-STARTs coverage keeps every 4th job unwoken; per-process temp files for parallel lanes. 150 clean runs in a row (4 and 6 lanes beside the live game). Open: the CPU-bound hang was not reproduced (0 in about 226 runs). Next: P5c may go live by the gate; sample threads on any future selftest hang.

**Replay memory and consumer holds, ts3native 0.35.12 on master 0.35.11, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.15, docs/51 §136, ~3 h):** `snap-p4-long` on 0.35.9 was correct (0 M1-M3, 0 mismatches) but the replay clone hit `ReplayCapMB` at ~13 min (131 / 134 MB), then every shadow was M4 `nover/off`. Fixed: the replay's pointer tables doubled forever under key churn (erased slots counted as used; verified offline, fake `fps` 1024 -> 4096 slots); one `RP_LOTS` batch in flight (each carries the whole map order, 2.5 MB on BP; queued batches behind the lagging consumer were the bulk of the growth, inferred); `plOverlap` = the sim's cut using the private pools during a `Mode=1` shadow, now one owner (`g_plSet`: the cut only tries, busy = deferred, P4 refused `noclone`); consumer `RpUnify` / map resync no longer rebuild tables of every copy under `+0x918`, GC every 16th hold; the cut cost no longer counts the replay audit hashing (BP 10.1 of 23.4 ms/s); rebases walk the map once less. `mat/timeout` should follow the lag (inferred). Selftest `TestReplayLongRun` + `TestPools` ok, steerro-test ok. Next: rerun `snap-p4-long` BP + IP on `game-copy/ts3native.asi.snap03512` on a quiet machine; pass: no cap switch-off in 20+ min, `plOverlap` 0, holds and `mat/timeout` down, 0 M1-M3. Still open: the whole-map order per batch and its resync (delta format next), P3 image cost in the cut.

**`snap-p4-long` 0.35.12 follow-up, ts3native 0.35.13 on master 0.35.12, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.16, docs/51 §137, ~3.5 h):** 0.35.12 fixed memory (BP 62.7 / IP 76.8 MB). (1) IP lot mismatch: the same lot `0x08F300536B484DF0` Q 1, clone 9 entries more, on 0.35.9 and 0.35.12; it stayed wrong until a whole rebuild because an audit mismatch forced nothing. Now a mismatched lot is rebased at the next cut, and the next hold logs the entries only one side has ("audit diff"), so the next run names the writer (still unknown). (2) BP M2 plan 43: the same door pair and costs as 0.33.1/0.33.3, now with `[OptsInit] Enabled=1`; the ctor and the hub compare `0x64FCE0` were checked statically, so the stack-hub cause (I-94-380) is refuted for this plan. Lot `0x6C110033` permission or portal data is the next suspect; not fixed. (3) `noclone`: the cut took the private pool set even with nothing to rebase; now only a rebase or build takes it (the batch-in-flight wait is unchanged). (4) Holds: EPOCH (115-130 ms) and full-batch (182-206 ms) frees of replaced clone memory now go to a grave freed in ~1 ms slices; a 5-minute log splits the consumer's lock time by record type. (5) Cut / sim cost: not changed (all-lot rebases after a P4 release with an unknown dirty set, P3 image builds). Selftest ok (forced rebase + diff, grave), steerro-test ok. Next: rerun `snap-p4-long` BP + IP and `snap-p3b` IP on `game-copy/ts3native.asi.snap03513`; pass: no repeated lot mismatch, "audit diff" lines, `noclone` down, no "record type 5 applied in" lines, 0 M1-M3 apart from plan 43.

**IP lot mismatch: live bounds in REM records, ts3native 0.35.18 (set at merge) on master 0.35.17, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.21, docs/51 §147, ~1.8 h):** the `0x08F300536B484DF0` Q 1 mismatch starts after a whole clone rebuild that follows a P4 "ApplyLotUpdate in a clone run" discard (IP 1856, 0210). `0x666010` skips leaves when the `+0x2C` answer misses the Q rect; the three classes answer stored box floats (`0x620E90`), the floor level cache (`0x80EF80`) and the parent's answer (child `0x806C50`) [verified]. The W2 pre-hook now carries live `+0x2C` answers in the REM record; the replay compares each with the copy's, logs both rects and forces the lot on a difference (I-94-479). Selftest reproduces the shape (3 clone-only entries, equal maps) and heals it; no false forces in the random runs; `steerro-test` ok. Next: one `ip-snap-p4-long` run: the difference line names which copy is stale.

**P5 / P5b / P5c ready for live, ts3native 0.35.19 on master 0.35.18, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.22, docs/51 §149, ~2.3 h):** audited against 0.35.12-0.35.18 (one batch in flight, pool owner `g_plSet`, clone grave, two-bucket unify, clone-run lot attribution, flat bounds, shell refresh, REM live bounds): one broken assumption. Mode=2 acquires the clone inside the job's `+0x918`, but the replay consumer applies the cut's batch only under `+0x918`, so a consumer not yet at the cut when the job took the lock made the acquire spin 50 ms with the lock held, refuse `mat/timeout` and plan with the game (and a consumer blocked in `Lock_Acquire` refuses the P5b window). Fixed: `P5PreLock` in ServeAsync waits, holding nothing, until the consumer is at the job's cut and idle (bounded 50 ms). Everything else verified unchanged (table in §22.22.1). Selftest `TestP5PreLock`; selftest 100 / 100 clean (50 on 0.35.17, 50 rebased on 0.35.18, 4 lanes). Queues (all `game-copy/ts3native.asi.snapp5`, counters 72, HitchAttr on): `snap-p5-shadow` (Mode=2, VerifyEvery=1: nothing taken) -> `snap-p5` -> `snap-p5b` (ReleaseLock + ReleaseGates) -> `snap-p5c` (FreeLock, hitch-attr env) -> `snap-p5c-ab` (`--ab routesnap`, clean night). **Next:** park the 0.35.19 asi as `game-copy/ts3native.asi.snapp5`, run the chain in that order, one BP + IP pair per step, stop on any crash / `Mode=2 off` / hand-over line; read `snap-audit-report.ts` (P5, P5b, P5c blocks and gates), `shadow-compare-report.ts` (P5 verify line), `hitch-attr-report.ts` (ms/s vs BP 68 / IP 73); checklist docs/94 §22.22.4.

**Replay cut split and signature mix, ts3native 0.35.20 on master 0.35.19, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.23, docs/51 §151, ~1.7 h):** 0.35.17 BP 0303 (clean): cut 9.3 ms/s, "lots (replay)" 6.8; of all cut time 35% are 15 whole clone builds (150-219 ms) asked by P4 discards (`PcRelease` `P4REL_DISCARD` after lazy stops, = `snPcDiscard` 15), 6% rebases, and ~0.6-0.7 ms per cut the per-cut scan. Now `RpSigMix` (two 32-bit lanes; any one changed DWORD changes it) 17-61 us, `RpQSig` only for rebasing lots, mirror by index while the live order holds; new 5-min line `snap replay cut split` (steps, quiet cuts). exNodes walk (lead b) never runs on BP/IP (no lots left out). Expected BP cut ~6-8 ms/s; stage 1 also needs the P4 discard rebuilds gone (I-94-484, P4 lane) and lot-named W6b. **Next:** `snap-p4-long` BP on the 0.35.20 asi on a clean machine; pass = cut + sim <= 5 ms/s; read the split line (signatures, quiet share).

**Routing round on the 0.35.22 BP runs: live-footprint W6b, shells past deferrals, bnm share, `ReplaySigMs`, `LotsUnlocked`, ts3native 0.35.23 on master 0.35.22, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.26, docs/51 §160, ~2.9 h):** BP 0444 / 0458 (`LazyKeep=1`): no fault after 10+ keep releases each (I-94-492: two clean runs); M2 1 / 0, M4 5.32 / 4.76%, cut + sim 5.27-5.96 / 4.32-6.37 cyc-ms/s. Shells-only rebases go out past a map batch in flight or a busy pool set (fewer deferred cuts, `noclone`). bnm: an equal hash-order walk shares the old image without the sort (bench 173 -> 40 us). New switches, default 0: `[RouteSnap] ReplaySigMs` (signature scan at most every N ms, unscanned lots keep their old signatures; 250 = trial, ~-0.8 ms/s) and `LotsUnlocked` (RP_LOTS applied outside `+0x918`, holding `g_rpUse` + the pool set: max hold ~11 ms instead of 45-55). Expected BP cut + sim ~3.7-4.4 cyc-ms/s on defaults, ~3.0-3.6 with `ReplaySigMs=250`; M4 ~2.5-4%. selftest ok (`TestBnmShare`, `TestReplay03523`, `TestPcLazyKeep` live W6b), steerro-test ok. **Next:** `snap-p4-long` BP (+ IP) on the 0.35.23 asi with `LazyKeep=1` (W6b live lines, no 82-lot batch, cost lines), then one BP each with `ReplaySigMs=250` and `LotsUnlocked=1`.

**BP door M2 diagnostic: `[RouteSnap] DoorDump` (default 0), log-only, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.27, docs/51 §162, ~1.5 h):** the door answer asks script only when the portal owner's lot graph has `+0x8F` set (`0x64F46A`); the clone's lot copies keep the `+0x8F` of their last rebase because `RpSig` and the P3 image leave it out (I-94-500, verified). Suspect for the recurring M2 at goal 1261.20 / 1758.27 (I-94-464): lot `0x6C110033...` clone 0 / live 1, so the clone never asks and walks door `...7D22F4D0` (I-94-499, inferred). The dump (p4door.h) on an M2/M3 or a goal in `DoorBox`: the unshared door ids, the live asks with `used`, every lot whose `+0x8F` differs (clone vs live at the plan vs live now), both sides' portal records, the door mirror. selftest ok (`TestP4DoorDump`). **Next:** BP `snap-p4-long` with `DoorDump=1 DoorBox=1260,1757,1262,1759`; if `f8fDiff` names that lot, add `g[0x8F]` to `RpSig` (one line, replay.cpp) and confirm in one BP.

**Lot `+0x8F` in `RpSig`, ts3native 0.35.24 on master 0.35.23, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.28, docs/51 I-94-499, ~0.5 h):** built ahead of the door-dump run: a lone `+0x8F` flip now changes the lot signature, so the next scan sends a shell refresh and the clone takes the live byte (`RpLotShell` copies the whole node). No event-driven mark (E11 has no lot key; `ReplaySigMs`=0 scans every cut). selftest ok (`TestReplaySigMix`: 6 lone flips, old signature blind, new one rebases once and the clone byte matches), steerro-test ok. **Next:** BP `snap-p4-long` on 0.35.24 with `DoorDump=1 DoorBox=1260,1757,1262,1759`: expect no M2 at 1261.20 / 1758.27, empty `f8fDiff`, `rpRebaseWhy[2]` near 0.35.23.

**0.35.21 crash fixed: freed A\* object on clone Qs after a lazy keep, ts3native 0.35.22 on master 0.35.21, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.25, docs/51 I-94-492, ~1.3 h):** BP 0419 (noisy) crashed 3.4 min in, after the first lazy-keep release: `0x664396` is the Q's A\* node-hash lookup (`Q+0x28`); the release dropped (freed) the A\* object but kept the clone, and the acquire only re-points Qs holding the live or current object, so the Qs kept the freed one and later runs wrote into freed EA heap (`0x4E5EC6` free-list unlink) [cause verified in disassembly + source; heap link inferred]. Also older pending cuts (parked / second slot) acquired before the rebase. Fixed: dropped object remembered and re-pointed at the next acquire (also the old WRAP path); a keep release refuses every pending cut; `[RouteSnap] LazyKeep` default 0 (off: every lazy stop rebuilds as before). Selftest `TestPcLazyKeep` (d) with negative control; `hook-sites.ts --check` ok (docs/68 row `0x80EF80`). **Next:** `snap-p4-long` BP with `LazyKeep=1`, two clean runs before calling it fixed; the rest of the 0.35.21 checks below.

**Cut cost round: P4 lazy keep, lot-named W6b / W3, quiet-cut reuse, REM bounds patch, cycle costs, ts3native 0.35.21 on master 0.35.20, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.24, docs/51 §153, ~3.2 h):** BP 0303: 35% of cut time was 15 whole-clone builds after P4 lazy stops. Disassembly: before `ApplyLotUpdate` its callers write only that lot (`0x82B810`: built and room bytes, then W4), the quadtree-deferral callers only hooked leaves / new objects / the Q's lot; so those two stop kinds put their lot in the dirty list and release `P4REL_LAZY` (lots rebased, A* + W' dropped, unwound Q pins `+0x48` cleared and their lots forced); other stop kinds still rebuild. W6b in a clone run names its footprint's lots from the clone map (was force-all, ~180 ms batches); W3 names its lot. `[RouteSnap] ReplayQuietMs` (default 0 = off; trial 250): cuts with nothing recorded skip the lot walk and signature scan (SYNC kept), full scan at least every N ms; the split line counts late catches. IP 0341 REM differences (256): `0x80EF80` level-box inputs that change unrecorded (child `+0x10` z: 0.2-2.0 widening; 0.001 box drift) now travel in the REM record and are patched into the copy before the remove (no forced lot); a differing child-list state stays forced. New 5-min "snap cut cost" line (wall and thread-CPU cut + sim) and cyc-ms/s on the replay split and consumer lines (QueryThreadCycleTime / 2.3 GHz nominal) for noisy runs. Selftests `TestPcLazyKeep`, `TestReplayQuiet`, `TestRemPatch`, `TestCycles`; selftest ok, steerro-test ok. Expected BP cut ~3-5, cut + sim ~4-6 ms/s. **Next:** `snap-p4-long` BP + IP on the 0.35.21 asi (correctness: 0 mismatches / M1-M3, "lazy stop released without a clone rebuild" vs "clone built ... p4clone.cpp" lines, no W6b no-lot lines, "patched to live" vs "not patchable"; cost from the cyc-ms/s lines), then one BP with `ReplayQuietMs=250` for the exposure line. Not done: bounded `RP_LOTS` slices (not cheap), other stop kinds.

The "forced by a write" lots (BP 389, IP 573 / 526) are `LotGraph_Build` (W4): STALE records = W4 count, ~89% from `PcPrepare`'s first use at a P4 START after a footprint change cleared `+0x8C`; every signature rebase had its Q list unchanged. W4's quadtree changes go through recorded W1 / W2 (new Qs through W3) [verified disassembly], so W4 and signature lots with an unchanged Q list now get a shell refresh (node, portals, rooms, hashes; the clone keeps its Qs, trees and map nodes); a shells-only batch walks no map and needs no resync. IP lot mismatch (0210, same lot, no "keeps leaves" line): the non-reaching remove case is left (I-94-478), not fixed; 0235 had none. `RP_LOTS` hold split not built (full batches should become rare); bnm not touched. **`snap-p4-long` 0.35.13 follow-up, ts3native 0.35.14 on master 0.35.13, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.17, docs/51 §138, ~3 h):** 0.35.13 BP passed (M4 4.00%, 0 lot mismatches); IP 0 M1-M3 but one lot mismatch and 8 grid image mismatches, both only with P4 shadows on (`snap-p3b` IP 0.35.13 clean). (1) Lot mismatch named (3 lot footprints x 3 leaves, flag `0x200`): W2 `0x666750` erases the map nodes but skips the leaf remove when the footprint's `+0x2C` bounds are flat or miss the Q [verified disassembly], so a clone copy with other bounds than live keeps leaves; now a replayed remove whose copy answers flat bounds forces its lot at the next cut and logs the bounds (trigger write still inferred). (2) Grid: one stale 56-byte row for 8 audits; snap.cpp unchanged since 0.35.12, so an unmarked row removal under P4 (inferred); a grid mismatch now rebuilds the whole grid at the next cut and logs both rows. (4) Consumer: INS records were 59-138 ms/s of the lock time (LOTS 5-23): `RpUnify` walked the whole map per insert; now two buckets (map keyed by footprint id, verified). The full-batch hold split is still open. (5) P4 release: W1/W2/W4 in clone runs now name their lot instead of forcing all-lot rebases; the remaining no-lot kinds are logged. **P3 image cost in the cut (stage-1 lane A), ts3native 0.35.15 on master 0.35.14, no counter change, offline, not run (docs/94 §22.18, docs/51 §139, ~2 h):** 0.35.13 counters put the world images at ~9.8 (BP) / ~10.3 (IP) ms/s of the cut plus reader tile builds 1.9 / 1.7: bnm and grid rebuilt at 86% of cuts, R 484 / 695 times. 3. **Destroys (`0x2000003`, 11.8% of the sim wait):** why a destroy waits on the job; can it be queued to the worker like design D (docs/84 §2.4) or deferred on the sim thread. **Studied (static) 2026-10-18, docs/88:** the key is our own S3b closure #4 (`RT_DESTROY` waits for the running job). Destroy touches no routing state (7 hops); the real dependency is object lifetime only: the job looks objects up with `FindById` (no AddRef) and `lock inc`s them, and the destroy's last `Release` can free them under it. Queue-to-worker and whole-destroy deferral rejected (managed free, script sees the object gone at once). Recommended **design G, destroy graveyard**: park one reference (`0x9395A0`), destroy now without the wait, release at the next IC point with no job (D's drain points); keep the wait for D-X records, a full graveyard, closing and non-sim threads. ## Resume here (2026-10-16 23:15 PHT hand-off; superseded by the section above)

**2026-10-17 session: focus on new features with big gains, starting with routing off the sim thread (Rodney).**
- G11 IP measured: movable 6.3 of 65.9 plan ms/s (BP 14.2 of 55.1), so both under the docs/18 gates. Built anyway under the offload rule (stable steps accepted at <= ~2% loss). Almost every window close is gate site `0x62EAB0` (lot table find-or-insert, ~1,860 outermost unlocked sim-thread entries/s); every other site together ~10/s.
- **H6 S3b + M1 built** (ts3native 0.11.0, counters v26, `[RouteGate] Enforce=1`, parked `game-copy/ts3native.asi.h6m1` sha `a942c27fc757`, selftest ok; details docs/60 §2.3 status). Not run in game. **Ran 2026-10-17 (BP, docs/60 §2.3 status):** stable after 0.11.1 (0 crashes, 0 watchdogs, 0 stuck Sims seeded), but the seeded off/on pair is a clear regression: iterations/s -25%, hitches +36%, p99 iteration 645 vs 303 ms; the sim thread waits for the jobs (pump 36 + gates 6.5 + destroys 5 ms/s) more than the 33 ms/s of route time it sheds. Stays off; IP not run. Resume only when the steering closers are solved (docs/81, docs/82).
- **H6 W (new, Rodney's idea): handle the window closers so routing can really leave the sim thread.** For each closer: (a) a sync read that needs its answer now cannot simply move to the routing thread (it would wait behind the job), so make it lock-free when it touches no job-written state (e.g. a lot-table hit) or answer it from a snapshot; (b) a write the sim does not wait on can be queued to the routing thread and applied there in order, so its wait goes with the routing; (c) anything that reaches managed code stays on the sim thread. Check each candidate's transitive deps (locks, waits, managed and remote calls, sim-only state) up to **4 hops max** (Rodney, 2026-10-17; raised from 2 for the steering line): hop 1 = the candidate's own deps, hop 2 = the deps of anything hop 1 forces to move too; per candidate a verdict (movable / movable with named extra moves / stays on sim); deeper chains are listed as open and decided after. Static study done (docs/80): a lot-table hit writes nothing shared, but the busiest `0x62EAB0` callers (Sim steering) already took `+0x918` in `RoutingWorld_ObstacleQuery 0x82EDB0` earlier in the same function, so a lock-free `0x62EAB0` alone moves little. Native G11 in the BP M1 run agrees: windows closed by a `+0x918` acquire 83%, gate sites 9%, quadtree writes 8%; movable 1.8% of plan time. **Next H6 W target: the per-tick, per-walking-Sim steering query `0x82EDB0`** (docs/18 try-lock / snapshot option): 2-hop dependency table, what a skipped or stale obstacle answer does to Sims (overlap risk), then a measured prototype. Lot-table options (docs/80 §4) only after that. **Steering study done (docs/81):** the query writes shared state under the lock too (quadtree pin counter `Q+0x48`, deferred insert/remove flush `0x666A40`, footprint refcounts), and the job makes the same writes, so no reader lock or lock-free query; a skipped answer reads as "nothing in the way" for one tick (no cache, no retry), a stale one is mostly right. Recommended design: caller-level try-lock at `0x85FD10`/`0x846B70`/`0x85B470` (skip or reuse last tick's object ids, max 2 ticks, then wait). Gain cap (Poisson model, inferred): ~5.8 ms/s BP / ~7 ms/s IP if steering is all of the 83%, ~2 ms/s if half. Below the 15/30 gates: probes first (`vfG11CloseBy` at the 5 steering call sites, per-site `+0x918` holds, `vfStSkipDry`, `vfQtCount`), build only if steering dominates. **4-hop study done (docs/82):** steering reaches managed code through the agent vtable (`0x1044590` `+0x60`/`+0x64` -> `World_FireEvent` 0x3A/0x39, after `+0x918` is released, so no deadlock, but a skipped check drops that tick's route callbacks); a 4th race (per-footprint polygon cache `[fp+0x38]`, rebuilt by both steering and the job); `node+0x30` is plan-time only; lock order clean to 4 hops. Changing only steering's writes (atomic counters, coarse quadtree lock, queue to worker) does not free it. Best design **E**: the worker takes our own lock exclusively only around its quadtree/footprint/lot-table writes (~14 worker-only detours), steering runs read-only (no pin counter, flush, refcounts or cache rebuild; invalid cache falls back to the docs/81 skip); 6-9 days; alone <= ~5.8 BP / 7 IP ms/s, with the docs/80 gate fix and quadtree readers 12-27 ms/s BP only if steering closes >= ~75% of `+0x918` windows. Unverified basis: a footprint stays alive while it is in the quadtree. **Next: probes first** (`vfG11CloseBy` by steering call site, then the 7 docs/82 §9 counters) in one observe-only build + BP/IP run; decide E vs stop after that. **vfG11CloseBy measured 2026-10-17** (0.11.2, seeded 12345, async off, `h6-m1.json` bp/ip-g11-closeby, both PASS): one call site, `RouteFollow_EditPath 0x85F5A0` -> `GoalFootprintGather 0x82F0B0` (return `0x85F6CB`), causes **94.7% (BP) / 96.9% (IP)** of the movable time lost; next are gate 10 (goal step `0x75C2D0`) 2.7% / 2.8% and quadtree reinsert 2.3% / 0.1%; the obstacle query `0x82EDB0` sites barely appear. Movable today 1.0 of 60.1 s (BP), 0.9 of 96.9 s (IP). **Next: a read-only `0x82F0B0` during a job for that caller (other sites keep waiting), shadow verify outside jobs, tripwires (pin counter, polygon-cache rebuilds by the sim during a job), fallback to waiting when unsure; plus a worker job queue so other Sims' plans stop waiting on the single slot.** **Job queue built and run 2026-10-17** (0.11.3, counters v28, `bp-h6-queue-seed-on` PASS, same seed as the M1 pair): 8,742 jobs async (single slot: 3,815), 8 refused, 1 dropped, 0 skipped, queue depth max 4, 0 watchdogs, `vfRtQtBare` 0, 0 stuck Sims. Vs seed-off: iterations/s 26.3 vs 28.4 (-7%; single slot -25%), hitches 5.1 vs 4.8/s, p99 iteration 307 vs 303 ms, sim-thread route 50 vs 110 ms/s; the sim still waits 62 ms/s pumping (the `0x82F0B0` path edit), 16 ms/s at gates, 8 ms/s on destroys. **Probe P0 (0.11.4, 2026-10-17):** sync G11: 95% of closes keyed `0x85FFC7` = the plan's own `Route_PlanCommon` tail (runs on the worker in async mode), so the docs/81-83 steering share was a measurement artefact; locomotion `0x85FEDC` is not a closer. Async (queue on, seeded, `bp-p0-async` PASS, 10,996 jobs, 3 dropped): the sim waited ~74 ms/s; by site: discourage footprints `IC_RouteManager_Add/RemoveDiscourageFootprint` -> `0x834370`/`0x834860` 29%, steering obstacle query `0x82EDB0` from locomotion `0x85FE10` + avoidance `0x846D21` 26%, `LotTable_Contains` gate 12 15%, destroys 12%, `UpdateSimFootprint 0x833560` 5%, goal step 4%. `bp-p0-sync` froze game time after its `save_game` step (save itself result 0); one occurrence, logged; rerun to check. **Next: static study (5 hops) of the top three, then build: queue discourage footprint writes to the worker, read-only steering query, lock-free LotTable_Contains hit.** **Design D built and crashed 2026-10-17:** 0.12.0/0.12.1 called `ObjectService_FindByIdAddRef 0x9395A0` with the wrong argument count (it is `(idLo, idHi, Object** out)`, `ret 0xC`): stack corruption, silent death 10 s in (no EA report; the new `[Crash] Log=1` exception logger exists since 0.12.1). Fixed in 0.12.2, which then crashed applying a deferred Add: AV reading `0xFFFFFFFF` at `0xAB88BD` under the lot lookup `0xC62D40` called from `0x834519` (inside `AddDiscourageFootprint`), from our drain. 0.12.3 keeps the flags as whole dwords (0.12.0 truncated them to bytes) and sets `[Discourage] Enabled=0` by default. **Cause found (static, 2026-10-17):** the fault is `movaps xmm0,[eax]` at `0xAB88BD` (`0xADD740` <- `0x834514`) on the first point of our copied array; `movaps` needs 16-byte alignment and the copy came from `malloc` (8-byte aligned on 32-bit), so a #GP reported as an AV at `0xFFFFFFFF` (matches the logged info). Not the flags, not the object's state. 0.12.4 copies with `_aligned_malloc(...,16)`; the selftest fake asserts alignment. **Design D confirmed 2026-10-17** (two clean runs `bp-d-async-3`/`-4`, crash rule): same build 0.12.4, seeded, unfocused, vs async off `bp-d-off-same`: iterations/s 36.7/35.9 vs 32.7 (+10-12%), hitches 3.1/3.2 vs 3.8/s, sim-thread route 50 vs 119 ms/s, 12,203 jobs 0 dropped, 0 watchdogs, `vfRtQtBare` 0, 0 stuck Sims, 1,676 discourage calls deferred and all applied. Regression: p99 of the per-second max iteration 285/286 vs 259 ms. The earlier '-7% with the queue' compared a focused run with unfocused ones (invalid). **p99 rise analysed (hitch logs):** hitches > 200 ms 14 off vs 15/12 on, same mean length, worst-5 lists overlap, so mostly which few seconds land in the top 1%; one real part: locomotion inside long hitches 21 vs 4 ms = the steering query `0x82EDB0` waiting for a running job (P0's 22% site, docs/84 §3 design S2); hitches > 100 ms -43%. Next (Rodney): (2) done, (1) fresh off/on baseline in the realistic test_env plus Isla Paradiso, (3) the EditPath freeze root cause. Rule learned: read the disassembly of every game function we call, not only the ones we hook.

**Key results:** thumbnail texture work runs on the sim thread (confirmed twice; crash lead, fix #4); render jobs run in the sim pump (#21); ThreadedObjects stalls up to 885 ms are single route searches (docs/64; H6 is the structural fix); the package probe walks ~140 packages per cache miss (#9); the object id hash is overloaded, chains 8-15 (#22); the 2026-10-14 crash is most likely our `[Alloc]` double-free bug (docs/67; keep `[Alloc]` off; alloc soaks held); lot adds are cheap natively; lot scoring is cheap while playing; the native sampler was broken since 2026-10-13 10:27 (all later `simstack.jsonl` empty) and is fixed but not yet confirmed in game.

**Rules added this session:** (1) mapping claims are confirmed live (verify counters, repros) or by offline input/output tests with block coverage (`tools/emu`) before fixes rely on them; (2) prefer our fixes where they overlap S3PP and win an A/B; turn the S3PP feature off in its ini, no S3PP fork (docs/69); (3) never hook inside bytes another mod searches for (docs/68; `node tools/hook-sites.ts --check`).

**H6 async routing build order (docs/60 §2, 2026-10-16):** S2b observe-only counters on all 15 gate sites + `vfRt*`; S3a terrain-swap source fix alone (parks the shape on `PhysShape_Release 0x682760` from the swap, frees on main when no ray in flight; also fixes the main-vs-sim race, fix #1); S3b all closures on in sync mode; S4 milestone M1 (async only in live mode, no build/buy/save, no pending lot updates, clean lot index); S5 lift limits one by one. Proxies replaced by worker-held locks (proxy = up to one sim tick). Hook sites shared with `[HeightCache]`/`[Verify]` need one shared stub per address.

**Rule (Rodney, 2026-10-16): mapping is not trusted until checked live.** After every mapping run: (1) add each runtime-checkable claim to the inference ledger (docs/51) with how to test it; (2) add the probe, counter or repro that can check it (`[Verify]` counters with the `vf` naming, agent actions + repros.json), or note why it is static only; (3) run the short `verify` queue and fill the verdicts (`verify-verdicts.ts --write`); (4) fixes build on claims that are confirmed by EITHER (a) a live check (verify counters, repros) OR (b) an offline test of the function itself by its inputs and outputs: run the original exe code (e.g. under an x86 emulator such as Unicorn on the TS3W.exe bytes, or in-process on recorded inputs) against our model or replacement, with measured basic-block coverage of the function (target: every block reached, plus edge cases) and differential output checks; pure or near-pure functions (hashing, RefPack, lot transform math, lookups, parsers) fit (b). Anything else is carried as a stated risk (Rodney, 2026-10-16). Open now: ledger rows for docs/52-62 (UI drawing, shaders, strings, input, Mono state/GC/caches, autonomy, routing closures, events, IC safety) are not added yet; the next verify build covers them (e.g. the 6 Mono warm-up counters from docs/58, worker-side `vfRt*` from docs/60 §2, IC safety spot checks of thread use).

B = snapshot built and scored on the sim thread as a Verify-only check: GO with conditions (0 mismatches over 10k searches on BP/SV/IP). **Emulator harness (tools/emu, docs/63, 2026-10-16):** `python tools/emu/run.py --self-check` passes on master: `HashStringFNV64_W 0x7CFE40` 312/312, `Lot_WorldToLocal2D 0xAB8540` 438/438 bit-exact (100% blocks), RefPack decode `0x4EB3B0` 212/212 (66/66 blocks). All three touch no `.data` globals for the tested inputs (pure; RefPack safe on any thread). For fix #11 (per-lot sin/cos cache): the cached sin/cos must be rounded to float32 to stay bit-exact; the `-s` term comes from the `-0.0` constant at `0xF988F0`. RefPack returns the header size even on a short stream and 0 for an empty resource (0 is not an error signal). Needs `pip install unicorn capstone`.

**ThreadedObjects stalls (docs/64, 2026-10-16, from existing samples):** slices over 200 ms are 80% single native route searches (`Route_Plan`), run by Sims doing travel interactions (go home, visit lot/community lot, rabbit holes, Go Here With); the 885 ms max is one 862 ms plan. Gangway re-plans (House Boat Fix + game exit path) are 33-38% of plan samples in long slices. Autonomy and SimUpdate are steady per-tick cost, not stalls. Next: cap only retried plans (DoRoute replans, plans to an already-failed lot), skip/reuse gangway re-plans (behaviour spec, no code copy), yield between plans in the ts3fixes `Route.Plan` hook; confirm with a per-task timer at the ThreadedObjects calls `0xD7FA74`/`0xD7FB53` (task id `host+0xBD0`). H6 async routing is the structural fix.

**Lot scoring (docs/65, 2026-10-16):** not a live stutter source: 15-30 µs per call while playing; the 30-116 ms calls are at world load (forced main-view draw `0x6F3C90` via `0xC69FF0` when `Renderer+0x29D` is clear). S3SS Throttle Lot LoD Transitions picks the cheaper branch (`[0x11ECBC0]=1` does the same without S3SS); S3SS also chunks world-load object adds via its `0xAC1130` detour (up to 34 chunks per pump pass, pump max 60 ms). Moving scoring to a worker rejected. `[LotStream]` lot-add µs is not valid with S3SS on (its detour bypasses our timer).

**Crash triage (docs/67, 2026-10-16):** crash A (2026-10-14 11:58, write to `0x694690`) is most likely our own `[Alloc]` cache: `EaFreeHook` caches a pointer freed twice twice, the bin links to itself and two owners share one chunk (bug verified in alloc.cpp; link to the crash inferred; `0x694690` is a device callback, no mod patches it, `0x0804xxxx` is executable game heap, not a mod stub). Keep `[Alloc] Enabled=0`; fix = drop duplicates in the free hook (walk the bin, max 32, count them) and treat count 0 as empty; confirm with an offline double-free selftest, then the alloc soaks. **The lane-G `bp-alloc-soak-1/2`, `ip-alloc-soak-3` jobs (the only lane-G jobs left) are taken out of the resume command until that fix is built.** Crash B (2026-10-15 15:38, `0x5ceb9a`): CAS model builder part teardown on main calls through a float (5.0) as a pointer: game-side lifetime race with ThumbnailManager / sim-thread CAS calls (inferred, fits the docs/43 thumbnail race); no mod or our code on the path; keep `travel_university` without `dialog: true`.

**Hook conflict map (docs/68, 2026-10-16):** S3SS and S3PP patch from their own threads after ts3native loads, so a shared site means ours installs and theirs silently fails. High: `[Verify]` Costs hook on `0xD7F8B0` disables S3PP's stage profiler and with it its type-lookup speed-up (verify timings are not comparable with S3PP on); planned fix #10 at `0xD81FC3` sits inside the bytes S3SS searches for its 480 TPS/60 FPS limiter: put the pump deadline in the existing `RunJobs` hook instead; fix #29 at `0x59C5B0` = S3PP text-lookup cache (pick one). Medium: MonoThread `GC_add_roots 0xE4D9F0` vs S3PP; `ObjScene`/Verify on `0xABFAC0`/`0x7D2DB0` vs S3SS lot streaming (check the S3SS log once). Rule for new hooks: never write inside bytes another mod searches for; `node tools/hook-sites.ts --check` keeps docs/68 complete.

**Decision (Rodney, 2026-10-16): prefer our fixes; no S3PP fork.** S3PP (GPLv3, sonja92yu) switches each feature in its ini, so where one of our fixes overlaps an S3PP feature and wins its A/B, the installer turns that S3PP feature off (queue field `s3ppIni`) and ships ours; S3PP features we do not replace stay on. Inventory done (docs/69): 9 S3PP 2.1 features; keep 4 (`MonoTypeEHashProbe`, `SimulatorStageProfiler`, `MonoIsinstCache`, `PackageIndexWarmup`), compatible 2 (`StblBucketLayoutTuning`, `AllocatorArenaTuning`), replace 1 (`LocalizedStringCache` by #29 after A/B: set `LocalizedStringCache=0`, also needed by the MonoThread `GC_add_roots` hook), off 2 (`ThreadStackDiet` until the docs/66 #2 A/B, `ShortWaitSpin` overlaps S3SS AdaptiveWait). #29 must read the active STBL layout at run time (S3PP shrinks tables from 7,919 to 509 buckets). Installer needs a conditional ini override (`configWhen`) when an overlapping fix ships off by default. A/B jobs A-E listed in docs/69 (profile jobs wait for the sampler-hang fix).

**s3pp-cache queue 2026-10-16: both jobs FAIL, but the game did not hang** (docs/data/results/2026-10-16-sampler-hang.md): every `/simstack` call has timed out since 2026-10-13 10:27 (all `simstack.jsonl` since then are empty, incl. the passing mainstack run); timed-out calls kept running in the agent and starved the watchdog heartbeat, so the queue killed a healthy game. Fixed (agent.js: sim thread id from `[0x11D8A8C]`, module list cached; ts3probe.py: one stack call at a time; profile-world.ts: counts lost samples). Rerun s3pp-cache to confirm; the first run must show Simulate frames in the samples.

Research with a sub-agent while the main lane works (static only; no edits under `mods/` or `tools/setup` while the game runs, per the parallel-agents rule):
  1. Script RNG: `Sims3.SimIFace.RandomGen.gRandomGen` (private static, from `AppDomain.GetData("RandomGen")`) -> `ScriptCore.Random` -> native `Random_NextDoubleImpl 0x74A780` (app vtable `+0x17C`, then `+0x28`). Find where the native RNG object is seeded (time source?) in Ghidra.
  2. List every `new System.Random()` in `game-copy/decompiled/` (docs/06 counted >= 59; default seed `Environment.TickCount`) and which ones run during sim play.
  3. Design the switch: ts3fixes `seed_rng(seed)` agent action that sets `gRandomGen` to a seeded `IRandom` and patches the default `System.Random` seed (or a native hook on `0x74A780` / the seed source); off by default.
  4. Then with the game: same save, same seed, N=3 runs seeded vs unseeded; compare sim minutes, route counts, autonomy picks; record remaining variance in docs/TESTING.md. Bit-exact is not the goal (thread timing stays).
- **M10, zoom out and pan:** max zoom, pan the town, log `commitMB` and largest free block; confirms distant lots stay imposters and how much lot streaming adds.

- ts3native 0.5.0 async route plan built and self-tested, not run in game (docs/18 "Async plan", docs/15 Phase 5): native cannot return "pending" (all 278 `Plan()/Replan()` callers need the result at once), so the ts3fixes `Route.Plan` hook (`PlanAsync`) sleeps the Sim's own task tick by tick while the worker runs the whole `IC_Route_Plan`; handshake via `GetOption` on unused RouteOption bit 58; blocking `Lock_Acquire` waits on the sim thread run the job's portal-lock managed calls. Opt-in `[RouteAsync] Enabled=0`, runtime switch `/native?routeasync=0|1`, repro `bp-route-async`, counters ts3fixes [52-54]. Test only after the offload-sync run is clean. 0.5.1 (2026-10-13): first in-game run aborted on `interp.c:3595 pState->yieldReturn` (yield from inside a native->managed call); START now refused unless the current interpreter state is the task's root state, and ts3fixes `MayYield()` (NOYIELD query, bit 58|61) guards the Overwatch and watchdog yields (docs/18 "Crash: yield in a nested call"). Not yet rerun in game. 0.7.1 (2026-10-13, self-tested, not run in game): the async assert/hang came from the host delegate call `0xD75FA0` running on the worker and zeroing the sim thread's task id (host+0xBD0); it is now proxied whole to the sim thread (detour), orphan jobs are dropped on CANCEL or mode flip, the lock pump also serves finite deadlines, counters v8 add plans/fails per mode (docs/18 "Crash: async route assert, hang, orphan job").
- ts3native 0.6.0 save write built and self-tested, not run in game (docs/15 Phase 7, offload candidate #2): the native save `FUN_00AB1030` writes the working copy `CurrentGame.sims3`, then copies the old save to `<name>.sims3.backup` and the working copy to `<name>.sims3` (CopyFileW + FlushFileBuffers per file, ~33 MB each in Bridgeport). Opt-in `[SaveWrite] Enabled=0` times every step (one `save:` log line, counters v6) and with `Mode=1` copies the old save on a worker during the package write, then renames it to `.backup` (hash checks, else the game's own copy). Package write + script serialization stay on the sim thread. Next: test E with Mode 0 then 1 (docs/15 Phase 7 test plan); no save-time numbers exist yet.
- ts3native 0.7.0 faster RefPack decoder built and self-tested, not run in game (docs/15 Phase 6): the one call to the decode core (`0x4EC065` -> `0x4EB3B0`) goes to `RefpackFast` (wild copies, same results); offline on 3.7 GB of shipped resources identical, 2.2x a C port of the game decoder and 1.8x S3SS `RefPackDecompressor`. Decoding is only 0.24% of sim-thread time in live mode (15 profiles), so the expected gain is small (loads: ~0.2-0.5 s); opt-in `[Decompress] Enabled=0`, runtime switch `ts3native_counters.decompressMode` (counters v7), `Verify=1` for the first run, counters per thread in `ts3native.log`.
- ts3native 0.8.0 EA allocator cache built and self-tested, not run in game (docs/15 Phase 8, docs/20 item 2): `0x4E5E40` is a dlmalloc 2.7 derivative with one `CRITICAL_SECTION` per heap, fastbins only up to 64-byte chunks, an unsorted-bin sort on every non-fast Malloc and a full fastbin consolidation before every request >= 512 bytes [verified: decompile]. A per-thread, per-heap cache of freed 16-1024 byte chunks in front of Malloc `0x4E69D0`, Memalign `0x4E6A20` (align <= 16, offset 0) and Free `0x4E5920` skips the lock and the core; cached chunks stay "in use" to the heap (headers and user bytes untouched), heap Shutdown `0x4E5CB0` is detoured so chunks of a dead heap are dropped, OOM flushes and retries, thread exit flushes. Footprint: no reservation, <= 4 MB of committed heap held. Offline: ~16 ns per free+malloc hit vs ~55 ns for an uncontended lock alone. Expected 4-10% of sim-thread time if the hit rate is high [inferred]. Opt-in `[Alloc] Enabled=0`, runtime switch `ts3native_counters.allocMode` (+0x108, counters v9). Next: BP bundle run, then in-load A/B on `allocMode` with `/simstack`.
- ts3fixes lot object query cache built, not run in game (docs/20 §4a, offload candidate #3, item 9): `SimIFace.Queries.GetObjects(Type, lotId)` keeps one result per (type, lot) per sim tick (copy on hit; cleared on a new tick, after 100 ms, on any `Simulator.DestroyObject`; nothing cached while paused). Hot callers: tomb-room route callback, local autonomy (`GetObjects<Sim>` per commodity), parked vehicles, auto lights. Opt-in `kSpatialCache`, switch `spatialcache`, counters [62-64]. Next: `profile-world.ts --ab spatialcache`; check both patches say `ok` in `ts3fixes.applied`.
- ts3native 0.9.0 faster water route search built and tested offline, not run in game (docs/15 Phase 9, docs/20 item 1b): the water JPS (`0x620560`, 7.8% of IP sim-thread samples) spends ~92% in its jump `0x61FE10`, which recurses once per cell and makes ~10 helper calls (two `div`s, a vtable callback) per cell [verified: decompile + samples]. `WaterJump` is the same jump as one inlined loop; only the call at `0x6207FB` is retargeted. `waterjps-test.exe` runs the game's own code (copied from TS3W.exe to its addresses) against it: 43.9M jumps on 1000 random grids identical incl. callback calls; 3.9x faster. Expected ~50 ms/s of sim-thread time in IP, nothing in BP/SV [inferred]. Opt-in `[WaterJps] Enabled=0 Mode=1 Verify=0`, runtime switch `waterJpsMode` (+0x134, counters v10). Next: IP run with boats/swimming and `Verify=1` (mismatches 0), then in-load A/B on `waterJpsMode`.

**Working notes for the next context:**
- Parallel agents while the game runs: they must not edit `mods/` or `tools/setup/` while a `cycle.ts --restart` or installer step can run (the restart rebuilds the mods from source); native/ is rebuilt only by `install --only native-patches`. To install only ts3native without rebuilding the mods: detach the probe (`/detach`), `taskkill /F /IM TS3W.exe`, `install --only native-patches`, edit `Game\Bin	s3native.ini`, `ts3probe.py serve --launch`.
- Probe: `/reload` swaps agent.js but can leave the server with a destroyed script handle; prefer a full `serve` restart. Frida 17: use `Process.findModuleByName(x).findExportByName(y)` (no static `Module.findExportByName`).
- repro.ts reloads the save unless `--no-load`; use `--no-load` for a second pass in the same load.
- Restart + quick check: `bash tools/harness/restart-verify.sh <names> <save.sims3> [tries] ["--off a,b --max-stuck N --minutes M"]`; restart + in-load A/B: edit and run `tools/harness/restart-ab.sh`. One agent command: `node tools/harness/ag.mjs <cmd> '<json>'`. GC counters: `node tools/probe/gc-read.mjs`. Native counters: probe `/native` (needs a probe restart, agent.js gained `nativeCounters`).

More unlocked lot state exists (0x6255B0, 0x62E420/0x63D7B0, inferred): native async needs a "routing gate" (owner waits at every routing entry), which leaves overlap only with non-routing work. Kept opt-in/off; worker mode 1 (sync) is stable (11,523 plans, 46,152 host calls, 0 crashes) but no speed gain. Next for route stalls: make searches cheaper instead (water JPS precompute 0x620560/0x61FE10, route budget revisit, fewer replans).

**How we test:**
- Each fix gets a repro: a trigger action in the agent (`place_sim`, `sim_go_home`, `sim_visit_lot`, `sim_route_to`) plus an entry in `tools/harness/repros.json`; run with `repro.ts run <id>`, several at once with `run-many` (areas 150 m apart, no shared Sims).
- `stuck-scan.ts` runs alongside and turns other stuck Sims into repro candidates (`probe-out/repro-candidates.json`).
- Runs that end with stuck Sims save `Repro-<tag>-<time>` for direct retests.
- Full per-world profiles (`profile-world.ts`, `profile-compare.mjs`, gate `run-check.mjs`) only at milestones; in-load A/B with `profile-world.ts --ab a,b --slice 2`; quick checks with `verify-shortcut.ts`.
- Save growth: `save-stats.ts <save> <later save>`.

**Rules from Rodney:**
- Repro-driven fixes; full runs only at milestones; bundle fixes per world; compare in one load with switches.
- Success = stuck Sims no worse than vanilla and at most one caused by a shortcut per 10-min run; don't chase every vanilla stuck Sim.
- Stuck Sims are solved (2026-10-13, many runs with 0 real stuck Sims): log possible cases in `docs/data/stuck-log.md` (how they occurred), no research or fixes unless one repeats often or a fix of ours caused it. Focus: stutter, FPS, performance.
- Stop a run on an obvious error or outdated code and fix first; watch every long run; update this plan with each fix.
- Teleports must not break what the Sim does next or strand it; each shortcut type must be seen working once.
- Parallel sub-agents for code and research; game tests stay serial; test-only helpers stay in the agent.
- Triage every crash against the change ledger; never enter credentials; never commit EA files, decompiled code, saves or captures (the repo is published on GitHub).

## Tools (all in the repo, Windows)

| Tool | What |
|---|---|
| `tools/probe/ts3probe.py serve [--launch]` | Frida probe on 127.0.0.1:47891: `status`, `memory`, `simstack` (sim thread native stack), **`managedstack`** (C# stack via interpreter frames), `interp`, **`rescache`** (resource caches), `read`, `string`, `agent?cmd=…`, `agentstatus` (incl. `worldLoaded`), `reload`, `detach` (always stop this way). After changing `ts3probe.py`, detach and re-run `serve` (attaches without relaunch). |
| `tools/probe/managed-sample.mjs [secs] [hz] [out]` | C# sampler: ranks self, task and inclusive methods. |
| `tools/frida/stutter-profile.js` | Native per-iteration profiler (routes, GC, finalizers, hitches): `python -m frida_tools.repl -n TS3W.exe -l tools/frida/stutter-profile.js -P '{"out":"<path>","hitchMs":50}' -q -t 600`. |
| `mods/ts3agent` | In-game agent: loader (mailbox kept across loads) + hot-reloadable plugin. Harness commands incl. game_state, load_save, save_game, auto_dialog, **autopilot** (holds speed, clears popups within a frame in any in-world state; speed `none` leaves the speed alone), keep_alive (+ repairs broken home objects), decline_requests, quiet_ui, catalog_search/buy_object/place_object. Build `build-agent.ts`, hot reload `reload-agent.ts`. |
| `tools/harness/cycle.ts --restart` / `--save X` | Restart (rebuilds the agent; clears the script cache only when agent source or Mods changed); load a save (waits on the world-loaded flag), end in live mode, ledger snapshot. Launch ~55 s, menu→ready ~37 s. |
| `tools/harness/new-save.ts`, `age-save.ts`, `watchdog.ts`, `crash-ledger.ts`, `offload-phase0.ts` | New save; aging with autopilot/keep_alive/declines; 1-min watchdog (stuck Sims, frozen clock, low memory; triage on crash); crash ledger; offload phase 0 variants. |
| `tools/setup/install-fixes.ts`, `check-conflicts.ts`, `fixes.json` | Fixes installer and conflict check. |
| `tools/ghidra/*` (`run-headless.sh DecompileTo.java <out> 0 <addrs>`), `internal-calls-map.ts`, `extract-assemblies.ts`, `dbpf.ts` | RE tools. Decompiled C# in `game-copy/decompiled`, native decompiles in `game-copy/analysis/decomp`. |
